# Bali v3 — plan archive

`docs/PLAN.md`'s finished history, moved here word for word on 2026-10-13 so the plan every session
reads holds the current state only (`docs/DECISIONS.md`, 2026-10-13). History, not required
reading: search it like `docs/DECISIONS.md`, by a step's id, a PR number or a date, and never read
it front to back. Where the two differ, PLAN.md is the current truth. Each section keeps PLAN's own
heading; a step's full line moves here once it is done, and the status line PLAN replaces goes to
the top of the first section. PLAN's history before 2026-10-05, its old "Now" and header line, is
at the end of `docs/DECISIONS.md` ("Archive: PLAN's old Now (to 2026-10-05)").

## The status line's past entries (newest first)

_Last updated: 2026-10-22 (Central) — A slide upload that sends nothing for 30 s stops (#436;
`docs/DECISIONS.md`, 2026-10-22), the owner's decision: one stuck on a half-open connection or Wi-Fi
dropping packets sat on a frozen bar until its 25 minutes. Its row now says "The upload stopped.
Check your connection and try again." with Try again, as SlidesStates draws a stop with no answer;
once the whole file is sent the bucket has 2 minutes to answer (the conductor's call, awaiting the
owner); a slow upload that keeps sending never stops, and the 25 minutes stay the outer limit. No new
words or board. Before it, the portal's tests drive the page (#434). Next on the Windows PC: the four
steps for sessions before the pilot's first class, which wait for the owner's word; on the Mac, the
phone's L8 and M8._

2, a student's poll names it and the bytes her download fetches match its SHA-256, and then the
teacher stops presenting. In the load gate six classes follow a lesson with a slide shown, on phones
and laptops, none refused; one class's devices now open together at the bell, beside the Starts, as
a push would bring them (five classes' took the Starts to their 1 s limit). Each device's polls are
counted, and every request kind has a time limit of its own. Phase 7's steps with no screen are
done. Next: the UI steps, once production backups are on (L0b) and the bucket exists (M0), both the
owner's. Before it, W2 (#333): the portal's student side, logic with no screen.
The home reads the age check first, a read that makes no account, so a sign-in that never set up
the app goes to the invite page and leaves nothing behind; `/v1/me` then tells a teacher from a
student. A failed read says so, with a Try again, never a guess. The lesson is read every 2 s while
the tab is in view, with its ETag; it stops when class is over, waits out a `429`, and stops at a
`401`, now the results poll too, so a sign-out happens once. An answer's id is minted after the one
Bali holds, so a laptop whose clock is behind still counts, and the answer shown is the latest Bali
took. The phone's chip is the server's own state, never greener than the teacher's grid, kept "as
of" the last read while Bali can't be reached. Next: M9 (Phase 7 steps). Before it, L6 (#332): the
portal's live question, logic with no screen.
Asking a question and closing it, with or without showing the answer, each come back as the question
now or a typed failure for the class page to say with a Try again, which resends under the same id,
so an answer lost on the way never asks twice. The results are read every 2 s while the tab is in
view, with the tag of the last read, so an unchanged count costs a header; a `429` waits its
`Retry-After`. Once the question is closed (by the teacher, the bell or a newer question) the poll
reads once more and stops, since a read at the bell can be one answer short. Next: W2, M9 (Phase 7
steps). Before it, M7 (#331): the phone's slide decks in BaliCore, logic with no
screen. The phone asks for a deck's link only when it hasn't fetched that deck this session, sends no
sign-in token to the bucket, asks for a fresh link once if the bucket refuses one (as it does an
expired one), and hands the slide view nothing until the bytes' SHA-256 is the slide's; a mismatch, a
refusal or no answer comes back typed for the view to say, with a Try again. The decks it fetched
stay in memory only, for that session: emptied when the session ends, gone at sign-out. Rode along,
L5's follow-ups: a second poll loop takes over from the first instead of doubling it, and a lesson
that is over never also says Bali can't be reached. Next: L6, W2, M9 (Phase 7 steps). Before it, L5
(#330): the phone's live lesson in BaliCore, logic with no
screen. `LiveLesson` reads what a running session shows every 2 s with its ETag, a `304` keeping the
last read and the student's answer, and stops once the session is over; a `429` holds it for its
`Retry-After`, a minute at most. The app runs it only while in front (L8 wires it). An answer goes as
one request, never through the outbox, under an id minted after the answer held even from a clock
behind it, and every failure comes back typed for the card to say, with a tap again. Next: L6, W2,
M7, M9 (Phase 7 steps). Before it, M4 (#329): decks in the privacy paths. A teacher's account
deletion, a school's disposal and the retention run now remove the slide decks they reach and empty
their titles, and the API's sweep deletes each PDF from the bucket by M2b2's 30-minute rule; a
preview touches neither. A deck added after the school's year end keeps its teacher named. A PDF
delete the bucket keeps refusing is tried again a minute on, then two, up to an hour apart, and from
its third failure in a row reported to Sentry. Phase 7's backend is done. Next: the logic with no UI
(L5, L6, W2, M7, M9; Phase 7 steps). Before it, M3b (#328): the slide in what students read, and a deck's
download, M3's second half. A student's poll now says which page of which deck the teacher shows
(its SHA-256 and the page, never a link), and the teacher's snapshot says it too, with the id of the
change that set it; neither shows anything once the lesson is over. `GET /v1/decks/{id}/download`
gives a link of a few minutes to the deck's teacher, or to a student only while a running lesson of
their class shows that deck; a removed deck is gone for everyone. The phone's contract (BaliCore)
reads both. Next: M4, decks in the privacy paths (Phase 7 steps). Before it, M3a (#327): what a
session shows, M3's first half (split by its size, as decided in advance). A teacher shows a page of one of their checked decks in their
class's running session, or stops presenting (`POST /v1/sessions/{id}/slide`), each change recorded
as `slide_shown` in the session's stream; a change landing after a later one changes nothing, so
the students never settle a page behind the teacher. A deck can't be removed while a running session
shows it (`409 deck_in_use`). Next: M3b, the slide in the student's poll and the teacher's snapshot,
and the deck's download (Phase 7 steps). Before it, M2b2 (#325): a removed or rejected deck's stored
PDF, and the deck sweep. Each upload URL a deck is given is noted on it, a retried create's too, and the PDF of a
removed or rejected deck is deleted only 30 minutes after the last one (the link's 5 minutes, and 25
for an upload already under way), by the removal itself or else by the sweep. The sweep also turns
away a deck left unchecked for 24 hours. A complete reusing a spent id is refused before the file is
read. Next: M3, slide state (Phase 7 steps). Before it, M2b1 (#324): a deck's check, M2b's first half (split by its
size, as decided in advance). `POST /v1/decks/{id}/complete` reads the upload no further than 25 MB,
checks its `%PDF-`, its length and its SHA-256, then PDF.js (`unpdf`) parses it in a worker thread
stopped at 10 seconds: the deck `ready` with its pages, or `rejected` with why, its object deleted.
One-way: a deck checked already is answered with its verdict. Next: M2b2, the upload URL's 5-minute
rule and the sweep's 24 hours (Phase 7 steps). Before it, M2a (#323): a teacher's slide decks, M2's first half (split by
its size, as decided in advance). `decks` (migration 0023) is written by the engine, each write's
`eventId` spent both ways; `POST /v1/decks` answers a pending deck and its 5-minute upload URL (more
than 25 MB refused before any URL), `GET /v1/decks` lists the teacher's own, and `DELETE
/v1/decks/{id}` removes one, then its stored object. With no bucket set up, every deck route is
`503 deck_storage_off`. Next: M2b, the check and the sweep's 24 hours (Phase 7 steps). Before it, W1
(#322): the presentation read carries the student's own phone in the session, as the teacher's grid shows it: focused, unlocked, Screen Time off, or silent
past 90 s without a check-in by the server's clock with when it was last heard; none while they are
not in the session, and once it is over. A check-in that changes nothing leaves the poll `304`.
A fixture for each state, which BaliCore decodes; the load gate's lesson devices open from 5 s
after the bell, off the Starts' connections. Next: M2, the deck endpoints (Phase 7 steps). Before it, L4b

presentation every 2 s with its ETag: no `429` among them, so the poll needs no budget of its own

disagree (L4a's WARN). L4 is done. Next: W1, the phone's state in the presentation read (Phase 7
steps). Before it, L4a (#320): a live lesson's student side through the API.

the engine, the routes left for L3b2 (L3b split by its size, as decided in advance). A draft is

why, Open owner items the owner's queue.

  `postgres prod` with no public proxy). Portal `https://bali-portal.vercel.app` (Vercel
  `bali-portal`). Cognito pool `bali-production`: sign-up for `@vanderbilt.edu` only (the
  Pre sign-up Lambda); review accounts by `AdminCreateUser` (`docs/APP-STORE.md`, 5.4).

Steps under "Phase 7 steps" |

### Phase 5 steps (cloud parts; one PR each; 👀 = owner looks at screenshots; 🔧 = owner in a console)

- **P1** Sentry in the API: off without `SENTRY_DSN`, student data scrubbed before it leaves — ✅ `apps/api/src/monitoring.ts`, `@sentry/node` started by `server.ts` when `SENTRY_DSN` is set: the error handler's 5xx (a 503 included), a failed sweep or start, and Sentry's uncaught-exception and unhandled-rejection handlers (the process still exits) report, never a 4xx refusal; an allow-list of Sentry's integrations (no HTTP or Fastify instrumentation, no breadcrumbs, no tracing), `dataCollection` all off, and `beforeSend` dropping the request, the user, extras, breadcrumbs and the transaction and cutting Drizzle's `params:` and Postgres's echoed row and input values from messages; the route template as a tag, the release Railway's commit, the environment `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT`. Test: `apps/api/test/monitoring.test.ts`. 🔧 Owner: `SENTRY_DSN`, `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT` (`docs/DEPLOY.md`). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (P1)
- **P2** Sentry in the portal, the same rules — ✅ `apps/web/src/lib/monitoring.ts`, `@sentry/browser` started by `src/instrumentation-client.ts` when `NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` is set at build time: uncaught errors and rejections (Next hands React's uncaught render errors to `reportError`), and the API client's 5xx and network failures as `ApiFailure` with the method and the path's template; never a 4xx, nor an `ApiError` rejected uncaught. An allow-list of integrations (no breadcrumbs, no HttpContext page URL, no sessions, no replay, no tracing), `dataCollection` all off, and `beforeSend` dropping the request, the user, extras, breadcrumbs and the transaction, and cutting URLs to their template and UUIDs to `:id` in messages and stack frames. A fake-transport test finds no class or session id, student name, unlock reason, invite code, token or form value in the envelopes, and no event for a 4xx. 🔧 Owner: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` (and `NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT`) on the portal's build (`docs/WEB.md`).
- **P3** 👀 Teachers register their block on the portal by its ID — ✅ a "Your block" section under the classes on the portal's home page (`apps/web/src/components/blocks.tsx`, logic in `src/lib/blocks.ts`): the teacher's live blocks, read by the new `GET /v1/blocks` (their own, oldest first, none soft-removed; a student `403`; S1 matrix rows; the OpenAPI snapshot regenerated), and a field that registers one by `POST /v1/blocks`. The ID is checked as BaliCore's `BlockTag` reads a tag (ten ASCII letters and digits, any case and spacing, sent upper-case) before a try is spent on it. The route takes no `eventId`: it is idempotent on the tag, so Try again resends as it was. Said under the field: another teacher's block (the route's one `409`), a malformed ID, too many tries (`429`, by its `Retry-After`), no connection or a 5xx. Tests: `apps/api/test/blocks.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/blocks.test.ts`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (P3)
- **P4** 👀 A public support and FAQ page; its support email is eshan.shah@vanderbilt.edu (owner, 2026-10-04) — ✅ `apps/web/src/app/support/page.tsx`, `/support`, linked from `/login` ("Help and questions"): read without signing in, no API call, rendered per request like every page (S4's CSP); no Sign out bar there (`PortalBar` skips it as it does `/login`). What Bali is in two sentences; for students: joining, tapping in (a tap before Start waits), Emergency Unlock (a second's hold, no connection needed, an optional reason), calls, FaceTime, Messages and SOS, the bell with the app closed, turning Screen Time off, and the teacher-sees lists word for word from `ConsentCard` (a test reads `JoinView.swift` and fails on drift); for teachers: the invite code (asked of us by email; once, 14 days), registering the block, running a class, reports; privacy in plain words with the policy "coming soon" (C2 writes it; since C2-web, 2026-10-05, a link to the two draft pages instead); the support email as a `mailto:` link. No legal claims. Rode along: dev's portal client `bali-web-dev` recorded (`docs/WEB.md`, `docs/DEPLOY.md`), and two old phone clients listed for deletion under Phase 6's owner items
- **P5** 🔧 A CI workflow that uploads a TestFlight build when the owner's App Store Connect API key secrets exist (manual dispatch; skips cleanly without them) — ✅ (the code half) `.github/workflows/testflight.yml`, dispatched by hand with the environment to build against (dev, the default, or prod, whose values the Archive step sets; 2026-10-05): a Linux job checks the three secrets and, any missing, says which and passes; on `macos-26`, XcodeGen as `ios.yml`, a Release archive signed through the key (`-allowProvisioningUpdates`), the build number the run's, then `-exportArchive` with `destination: upload` sends it to TestFlight, no new dependency. A release guard (#130; part of S10) fails first on a `DEBUG` flag in Release, Debug-only text in the app or the monitor, an empty or non-HTTPS sign-in setting, or no app icon (the icon landed 2026-10-04: concept A, the mark on its stone-50 tile). Actions pinned by SHA, `contents: read`, timeouts, the key removed at the end. 🔧 Owner: the App Store Connect app record, an App Manager API key, and the secrets `APP_STORE_CONNECT_KEY_ID`, `APP_STORE_CONNECT_ISSUER_ID`, `APP_STORE_CONNECT_KEY_P8` (`docs/DEPLOY.md`, "TestFlight"). **The first prod build (2026-10-05):** run 1 failed on the key (its PEM refused; the owner re-pasted the secret); run 2 on the icon guard's false alarm (#235); run 3 uploaded and App Store Connect refused it (the NFC entitlement listed NDEF, ITMS-90778; the extensions had no `CFBundleDisplayName`, ITMS-90360), fixed by #236 (the entitlement TAG alone, the extensions named, both checked by `ios.yml` and the release guard); run 37279657642 (build 4) uploaded. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (P5)
- **P6** Owner runbooks: the production Railway environment, Cognito hardening for prod (Phase 6's list), the Vercel flip, the backup-restore drill — ✅ [`docs/RUNBOOKS.md`](RUNBOOKS.md), five numbered runbooks, each step with where to click and a check: (1) production on Railway — the environment, its Postgres on the private network with no TCP proxy, daily backups, every variable and how to make each secret, the backup sweep cron, 2FA, `/healthz`; (2) Cognito for production — a new pool, self sign-up off or gated, PKCE-only public clients with no password flows, a password policy, user-existence errors prevented, MFA, threat protection, exact callback and sign-out URLs, unused clients deleted, CloudTrail, MFA on the AWS root, and Sign in with Apple only if Google is offered; (3) the Vercel flip — the existing project kept building only `v2-archive` first, a new project rooted at `apps/web`, the domain, previews protected and never on prod's values, then `vercel.json` removed; (4) the restore drill — prod dumped into a scratch Postgres inside its private network, counts and one session's events compared, timed, the copy destroyed, and Railway's in-place restore practised on dev; (5) GitHub — CODEOWNERS and the ruleset, secret scanning with push protection, private vulnerability reporting, CodeQL, fork-workflow approval, the load gate required. Railway's point-in-time recovery is unconfirmed: the runbook says to raise it if the console has none (hosting decision 4). 🔧 Owner: follow them; record the drill's result here, on this line. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (P6)
- **P7** 👀 App Store drafts: description (student-focus framing, ISSUES #3), review notes, privacy-label and age-rating answers, options for how App Review tries Bali without a block (#130) — ✅ (drafts) [`docs/APP-STORE.md`](APP-STORE.md): name, subtitle, promotional text, description and keywords framed as the student managing their own focus (never "schools stay in control"), the teacher-sees lists word for word from `ConsentCard`; review notes (`.individual`, `.all()` shields, Emergency Unlock always, calls/FaceTime/Messages/SOS, the bell with the app closed, deleting the app); the App Privacy table (email, name, user ID, install ID, product interaction, unlock reasons, class membership; App Functionality, linked, no tracking) and the age-rating answers, ⚖️ marks for the lawyer; three ways for App Review to try Bali without a block, recommending a recorded tap plus review accounts. 🔧 Owner: decide the review path (section 4), then fill the accounts and paste.
- **P8** Railway's config-as-code moves from `railway.json` to `.railway/railway.ts` before 2026-12-01, when Railway stops reading `railway.json`. ✅ (the code half, 2026-10-06) `.railway/railway.ts` written by hand, not by `railway config migrate` (whose dry run in an unlinked clone named everything `bali-railway` and left the Dockerfile build commented out): `railway.json`'s settings (the `Dockerfile` build, `/healthz`, restart on failure ×3), the repo as source, every API variable as `preserve()` (an apply deletes a declared service's variables the file leaves out), one service per environment (dev `bali`, production `bali prod`, any other environment refused), scoped by the named partial `bali-api` so the Postgres services and the sweep crons are never claimed or deleted. The `railway` SDK is a root devDependency (the CLI evaluates the file with it); `tsc -p .railway` in `npm run typecheck`; `infra/railway/iac.test.mjs` checks what each environment declares and that every variable `apps/api/src/env.ts` reads is preserved. **`railway.json` stays** until the owner's apply is verified. 🔧 Owner: runbook 8 (`docs/RUNBOOKS.md`) on dev, then prod (`railway config migrate cutover`, `railway config plan` pasted to a session, `railway config apply`, a deploy from the `Dockerfile` and `/healthz` checked); then a session's follow-up PR deletes `railway.json` and its mentions (DEPLOY, runbooks 1 and 5). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-06, P8) Production applied 2026-10-06 (`bali prod`): the plan turned off "Wait for CI" (`source.checkSuites` true → null), since the file left it out; the file now declares `checkSuites: true`, and the owner re-runs `railway config plan` / `apply` on production to turn it back on (dev next, same commands). Production's deploys then failed their healthcheck (2026-10-06): Railway runs a Dockerfile service's start command without a shell, so `npm run migrate && npm start` ran only the migration. Neither file sets a start command now (the Dockerfile's `sh -c` CMD runs both; `railway.json` dropped its `startCommand` too, so dev matches); the owner re-plans and re-applies production, then redeploys. ✅ **Done (2026-10-06/07):** the owner applied the file to production and dev (#277, #278; both deploy Active, `/healthz` answers; plans show Wait for CI on, the `Dockerfile` build, `/healthz`, restart ON_FAILURE ×3, no start command; CLI 5.63.1 has no `migrate cutover`, and plan → apply worked without it). The follow-up PR deleted `railway.json` and its parity test, rewrote runbook 8 as the plan → apply loop (an apply resets any setting the file omits), and made the `Dockerfile`'s `CMD` `sh -c "npm run migrate && cd apps/api && exec node --import tsx src/server.ts"`: `exec` makes the server PID 1, so Railway's SIGTERM reaches `makeShutdown` (sh as PID 1 ignored it; #278's review).

- **P9** The sweep's Sentry Cron monitor and the owner's alerts — ✅ `withSweepMonitor` (`apps/api/src/monitoring.ts`) wraps each minute's run in `startSweeping` in a check-in to `api-sweep` (`in_progress`, then `ok` or `error`; the schedule, a 1-minute interval with a 2-minute margin, a 5-minute max runtime and 2 failures to an issue, rides along so Sentry makes the monitor); a plain call without `SENTRY_DSN`; tested with the fake transport (`apps/api/test/monitoring.test.ts`). A failed run is still reported as an error too. `/healthz` unchanged. [`docs/RUNBOOKS.md`](RUNBOOKS.md) runbook 6: Sentry alert rules for new or returning errors, the monitor's notification, an outside uptime check on `/healthz`, and a test alert. 🔧 The owner sets them up.
- **P10** 👀 Portal: the class page's session length and Extend, on the APIs that already existed (the owner's picks, 2026-10-05; D2 on hold, so today's Tailwind look) — ✅ `apps/web/src/app/classes/[id]/page.tsx`, the logic in `src/lib/session-controls.ts`, tested. **Session length:** presets 25, 50 and 75 minutes and Other… (any whole number from 1 to `MAX_SESSION_MINUTES`, checked by the page before a Start is sent, `NOT_A_SESSION_LENGTH` said under the field; the form is `noValidate`, so the browser's own bubble never speaks for Bali), the Start button saying the length ("Start 50-minute session"); the class's last pick remembered on that computer (`localStorage`, keyed by class id, every read and write in try/catch, the page working without it), 50 when none is. **Extend:** "+5 min" and "+10 min" beside End while a session runs, each sent under a UUIDv7 `eventId` the portal mints (`newEventId`); an answer that never came is resent under the same id by Try again, or by the same button (`extendAttemptFor`), and a new amount gets a fresh one; the bell ("Ends at 9:30 AM") shows beside the grid from the Start's answer, an extend's, or the grid's snapshots (`LiveGrid`'s new `onSession`, so a reload has it), and only ever moves later (`laterBell`: a window never shrinks). Past the bell the API refuses it (`409 session_not_running`, decision 12), said in words keyed on the reason: End it, then start a new one (an End in that gap ends it at its bell and shows the recap). Every failure said where it happened, with Try again (`CANT_REACH`, a 429's wait, `CANT_ADD_TIME`). Tests: `apps/web/src/lib/session-controls.test.ts`, `errors.test.ts`. `docs/PILOT.md`'s day-of-class checklist says how to use them. 👀 Screenshots: `~/bali-notes/class-controls/{before,after}/`.
- **P11** 👀 Portal: a new join code — ✅ (#247, landed 2026-10-05) a "New code" button by the code (`apps/web/src/components/join-code.tsx`), a confirm step ("Make a new code? ABCDE stops working right away.", Make new code / Cancel), then the new code in its place and said (`NEW_CODE_MADE`; `PATCH /v1/classes/{id}`, `regenerateCode` in `src/lib/session-controls.ts`, tested). Every failure said where it happened, with Try again (`CANT_REACH`, a 429's wait, `CANT_MAKE_CODE`); the route takes no `eventId`, and a resend after a lost answer mints once more, the code shown always the server's. Its buttons are the class page's own (today's Tailwind look, D2 on hold; the focus ring is D2c-1's global one). **Parked 2026-10-05 as a draft PR, unblocked by #255:** the API's CORS answered every preflight with `GET,HEAD,POST` (`@fastify/cors` 11's default `methods`; `apps/api/src/app.ts` registered it with none), so a browser was refused the `PATCH` before it was sent, on prod too, and the control failed honestly ("Couldn't reach Bali") every time; found on the rig. #255 names `methods` GET, HEAD, POST, PATCH, DELETE (the portal api-client's, PUT left out as iOS-only), with a preflight test per method in `apps/api/test/cors.test.ts`. `docs/PILOT.md`, setup step 5, says how to use it. 👀 Screenshots: `~/bali-notes/class-controls/after/new-code-*` (the confirm, and the CORS refusal before #255) and `~/bali-notes/class-controls/new-code-live/` (the code changing for real, on an API built from main after it).

(Its owner-only line is in `docs/PLAN.md`'s Open owner items, as "Phase 5's owner list".)

### Phase 4 steps (one PR each; 👀 = the owner looks at screenshots, 💻 = needs the owner's computer)

Planned 2026-10-04: the API's checks and rate limits first, then reports, then teacher
invites; the exit check last. Each step is one PR from a fresh worker, under ~400 changed lines not counting tests; a step that grows past that is split.

- **O1** OpenAPI snapshot: every route and the request schema it parses, in a checked-in file generated from the app; CI fails on drift, and on any removal (additive-only) — ✅ `contracts/openapi.json`, from the route table and the zod schemas each route declares (`config.parses`), rewritten by `npm run fixtures`
- **S1** Phase 6's authorization matrix, run here so the new report and invite routes are covered from their first PR: every route against six callers (no token, another app's token, a student, another student, a teacher who isn't the owner, the owner); a route with no row fails — tests only — ✅ `apps/api/test/authorization-matrix.test.ts`, over the route table O1 reads (each HEAD as its GET); `/internal/*` against no key, a wrong key and the right key, `/healthz` anyone; a 2xx to a stranger names nothing of the classroom; no authorization bug found
- **O1b** The two checks' gaps, from their reviews — tests and CI only. O1's: a deleted or regenerated-from-nothing snapshot can't hide a removal, and a test that every route parses through `parseRequest`, so none stays out of the snapshot. S1's: another student's history is read for the class's ids (its row answered `400`, so the stranger check never ran there), and a route outside `/v1`, `/internal/*` and `/healthz` fails the suite, never public by default — ✅ CI puts the base branch's copy of `contracts/openapi.json` in place before it regenerates, and a missing snapshot fails the test; a syntax scan of `apps/api/src` finds no request part read but by `parseRequest`; the history row asks for the first page; `callersOf` knows `/v1`, `/internal/*` and `/healthz` only
- **L1** Rate limits (ISSUES #1): a budget per verified account on signed-in routes, never per address; a generous per-address budget only where no one is signed in; a tighter per-account budget on guessing join codes (the preview and the join); `429` in the one error shape with `Retry-After`; the client's address read correctly behind Railway's proxy. From the security investigation: accounts are free while self sign-up is on, so per-account budgets alone don't stop code guessing — join-code misses also get a per-address or global backstop; `trustProxy` is set to Railway's exact hop; an unlock's `429` stays "retry" (the unlock contract) — ✅ `apps/api/src/limits.ts`, token buckets in each process's memory (`BUDGETS`), no new dependency: 120 at once and 120 a minute per verified account, spent in `authenticate` once the token checks out; 1,200 and 600 a minute per address with no sign-in or a refused one; on the preview and the join, 20 tries and 2 a minute per account and 100 misses and 6 a minute per address, a miss held through its lookup; `429` in the one shape with `Retry-After`; `/healthz` and `/internal/*` spend nothing. Railway documents no hop count, and Fastify 5.12 ignores a numeric `trustProxy`, so it stays off and the address is the edge's `X-Real-IP`; an extra `X-Forwarded-For` entry picks nothing. Sizes and why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **L2a** A load harness: the API run against a real Postgres with a local token issuer and a seeded school (600 students behind one address, their classes and blocks), for the gate to drive — ✅ `npm run load:seed` makes the database `bali_load` on this machine's Postgres (`TEST_DATABASE_URL`, refused unless local): 20 teachers with a block each, 30 classes, 600 students enrolled where they tap; it writes `apps/api/scripts/load/school.json`, each one's token, tag and class. `npm run load:serve` runs `src/server.ts` on it, trusting the seed's key through env alone (`AUTH_JWKS_URI` a `data:` URL; no API change). CI's real-Postgres lane seeds it, boots it, and taps a class in, every answer `200` under L1's budgets (`apps/api/test/load-harness.test.ts`). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **L2b** The one-address load gate (k6, in CI): that school taps in within one minute with zero `429`s, while one flooding account is stopped; it also times the sweep at that size — ✅ `apps/api/scripts/load/gate.js` and CI's "Load gate" (`.github/workflows/load-gate.yml`), on PRs touching `apps/api/`, `packages/db/`, `packages/shared/` or the root manifests: measured, 3,366 school requests from one address with no `429` and no `5xx` at p95 8.5 ms (bound 500 ms; a Start's, 1 s), the flooder refused 2,762 times with `Retry-After`, and the sweep marking 600 phones silent in 1.2 s and ending 30 sessions at once in 91 ms with no deadlock (`npm run load:sweep`). The harness now needs `LOAD_LOCAL_POSTGRES=1`, and `dropDatabase`, `recreateDatabase` and `makeTestDb` refuse in production. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **L3** Bound the sweep's per-row deadlock retry (a shared retry budget per run, or batching), only if L2b shows it is needed — not needed at this size (L2b): the sweep takes ~2 ms a row, and no deadlock met it; it comes back at ~30,000 phones going quiet at once on one API, or if the gate's deadlock count leaves 0 (CI warns)
- **R1** Focus time as one shared pure function: a session's events → its class focus minutes and its unlock list, by the reports row's counting rules (Go-live features) — ✅ `sessionReport` in `packages/shared/src/report.ts`: the session's window, its stored events and the server's `now` → who joined, the class's focus minutes (total, and the average per student who joined, null when none did), `silentMinutes` apart from focus, and the unlock and protection-off lists with their notes, oldest first; read in `seq`, the engine's order, a noted record moving no one; times held to the window the session really ran, and never before the student's record ahead. Tested on events built by hand, and against the real engine (`packages/db/test/report.test.ts`). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **R2** `GET /v1/classes/{id}/reports/sessions/{sessionId}`: one session's report — who joined, the class's focus minutes (total and average), the unlock list; aggregates only, never rankings — ✅ `apps/api/src/routes/reports.ts`, the class's own teacher only: R1's `sessionReport` over the session's events (`getSessionEvents`, each with its student's display name now), so a student removed since is still named; who joined, focus minutes (total, and the average per student who joined), silent minutes, whole minutes each rounded from its exact figure (the total never a sum of rounded parts), and every unlock and protection off with its note; a session not over yet answered to now or to its bell with `ended: false`; `403` another teacher or a student, `404 session_not_found` a session not in the class, `404 class_not_found` an unknown class, `400` a malformed id. A note this build doesn't know moves no one and is listed as none (#191's review); `makeAuthedApp` gets budgets no test meets (L1's review). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **R3** `GET /v1/classes/{id}/reports/sessions`: the class's past sessions, newest first, paged, each with its totals — ✅ `apps/api/src/routes/reports.ts`, the class's own teacher only: every session of the class, the running one too, newest first by `started_at` then id, each with its window, R2's `ended`, how many joined, the class's focus minutes (total and average), silent minutes, and how many unlocks and protection offs — R2's figures, counted on read by the same `sessionReport` over every event the session holds (the page's in one statement, `sessionEvents`) and made whole by the same function, a test reading both for every row; 20 a page (`SESSION_REPORTS_PAGE_LIMIT`), `limit` asking for fewer, `before` the last session's id and `nextBefore` the next, through a new index (`sessions_class_started_idx`, its plan pinned); `400 unknown_cursor` a `before` the class doesn't hold, `400 invalid_request` a malformed request, `403` another teacher or a student, `404 class_not_found` an unknown class — now on every route under `/v1/classes/{id}` (`requireOwnClass`, #192's review). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md`
- **R4** 👀 Portal: the recap card when a session ends — ✅ `RecapCard` (`apps/web/src/components/recap-card.tsx`) on the class page: the class's newest session once the server has marked it over (the End's answer; the grid's `session_ended` or `session_expired`, or a snapshot saying `ended`; on a fresh visit, R3's newest row), until a new session starts — the day and times it ran, who joined, the class's focus minutes (total, and per student who joined), silent minutes, every unlock (who, when, reason) and every protection off; loading, an error with Try again, nobody joined and no unlocks each said; aggregates only. The logic in `apps/web/src/lib/recap.ts`, tested. Ride-along (L1's review): a `429` reads in the phone's words (`apps/web/src/lib/errors.ts`; its wait "a moment" since R5). 👀 screenshots sent to the owner. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (R4)
- **R5** 👀 Portal: the reports page — past sessions, each opening its recap — ✅ `apps/web/src/app/classes/[id]/reports/page.tsx`, "Reports →" on the class page: the class's sessions the server has marked over, newest first, from R3, 20 a page with Show earlier; each row when it ran, how many joined, the class's focus minutes (total and average), silent minutes, and how many unlocks and protection offs (`classFigures`, the card's own words); a row opens its recap, R4's `RecapCard` fed by R2; loading, a failure with Try again, no reports yet, and a stale cursor (`400 unknown_cursor`, now `ApiError.reason`: the newest page read again, and said) each said; aggregates only. The logic in `apps/web/src/lib/reports.ts`, tested. Ride-alongs: the class page keeps the ended grid under the card until the next Start, drawn once from the ended session's snapshot (R4's review WARN, the owner's default); a `429` says to wait a moment (R4's review WARN); the owner's rulings of 2026-10-04 recorded. 👀 screenshots sent to the owner. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (R5)
- **T1a** Teacher invite codes, stored, and the owner's command to mint one for a school. Its rules, the owner's (2026-10-04): single-use, expiring 14 days after minting, each minted by the owner's command; an account already a student in a live class can't redeem one, and is told to use a separate account for teaching (`docs/DECISIONS.md`). From the security and compliance investigations: single-use, expiring, stored hashed, rate-limited; minted only for a school with a signed data agreement — ✅ `teacher_invites` (migrations 0012 and 0013, the second a trigger refusing any change to a redeemed row) and `schools.agreement_signed_at`, a `date`; the owner's `npm run school` (`apps/api/scripts/school.ts`, its logic `packages/db/src/schools.ts` and `school-command.ts`): `add <name>`, `agreement <school-id> <day>`, `invite <school-id>`, against `DATABASE_URL`. A code: 25 symbols of `JOIN_CODE_ALPHABET` (≈124 bits), printed once as five groups of five, stored as its SHA-256 only (unique; a `CHECK` admits 64 hex digits alone), a collision drawn again up to 8 times, then refused with nothing stored; `expires_at` the database's `now()` plus 336 hours; the redeem columns whole or none, `redeem_event_id` unique, ready for T1b's guarded UPDATE; a school with no agreement on record, or none at all, refused, the mint holding the school's row from its read to its write. Tests: `packages/db/test/schools.test.ts`, and two real-Postgres races in `races.test.ts` (two agreements recorded at once; a mint behind a change holding its school's row). The rate limit is the redeem's, T1b's. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (T1a)
- **T1b** Redeeming an invite code makes the account a teacher at the code's school in one step, idempotent on its `eventId` — ✅ `POST /v1/teacher-invites/redeem` (`apps/api/src/routes/teacher-invites.ts`, its logic `redeemTeacherInvite` in `packages/db/src/schools.ts`): `{ code, eventId }`, the code matched as the command printed it, its case, spaces and any dash set aside (`inviteCodeSymbols`), and looked up by its hash; in one transaction `users.role` and `school_id` set and the invite's redeem written by T1a's guarded UPDATE, the caller's row held to the end; answered `{ outcome: 'redeemed' | 'replay', user }`, the user as `/v1/me` gives it. Each refusal a new reason, nothing changed: `400 invite_code_invalid`, `404 invite_not_found`, `409 invite_used`, `409 invite_expired`, `409 already_teacher`, `409 student_in_class`; another account's eventId `409 event_id_conflict`. An account with no row yet gets one as the boot call makes it. Rate limits: 5 tries then 1 a minute per account, 20 misses then 2 a minute per address (`inviteTries`, `inviteMisses`). Migration 0014: a redeemed invite can't be deleted either (T1a's review WARN). The authorization matrix and the OpenAPI snapshot carry the route. Tests: `apps/api/test/teacher-invites.test.ts`, `rate-limits.test.ts`, `limits.test.ts`, and three real-Postgres races in `races.test.ts` (two accounts, one code; one account, two codes; two accounts, one eventId). `npm run school -- list` and a warning on a duplicate school name (T1a's smaller WARNs) were left out for size. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (T1b)
- **T2** 👀 Portal: a signed-in account that is not a teacher yet enters its invite code — ✅ `InviteCode` (`apps/web/src/components/invite-code.tsx`) on the home page, in place of the classes view for an account `GET /v1/me` says isn't a teacher: the code typed or pasted in any case, with or without its dashes or spaces, shown in fives as the command printed it, the caret kept; checked by the redeem's own rule before a try is spent (`INVITE_CODE_PATTERN`, now in `@bali/shared` with `JOIN_CODE_ALPHABET`, `INVITE_CODE_LENGTH`, `inviteCodeSymbols` and `formatInviteCode`, `@bali/db` re-exporting them); redeemed under a UUIDv7 `eventId` the portal mints (`newEventId`, no dependency), an attempt whose answer never came resent under the same one, a refusal or a new code a fresh one; success reads `/v1/me` again and shows the classes, their heading focused, no reload; each refusal in its words keyed on `reason` (`errText`, tested): `invite_code_invalid`, `invite_not_found`, `invite_used`, `invite_expired`, `already_teacher` (Go to your classes), `student_in_class` (a separate account for teaching), a `429` (wait a moment), no connection (Try again); a failed boot read gets Try again. The logic in `apps/web/src/lib/invite.ts`, tested. Ride-alongs: R5's review (`readSessions`: Show earlier at the end reads nothing; a failed stale-cursor restart keeps no cursor), T1b's review (ARCHITECTURE.md: a refused redeem can fill a missing display name, as `/v1/me` would; a test pins it). 👀 screenshots sent to the owner. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (T2)
- **T1c** The join refuses an account that became a teacher, inside its own transaction (T1b's known edge) — ✅ `joinClassByCode` (`packages/db/src/transitions.ts`) reads the caller's `users` row FOR SHARE before its class and answers `not_a_student`, writing nothing, for an account that is no student; the route (`apps/api/src/routes/enrollments.ts`) refuses it with the join's own `403`, unchanged, and no longer judges the role before the transaction. The redeem holds that row FOR UPDATE, so a join and the same account's redeem run one at a time: the join first, and the redeem is `409 student_in_class`; the redeem first, and the join is refused as a teacher's. Never both. The row before the class, as a rename takes them; shared, so the foreign-key checks of the student's taps and unlocks, and of a Start's conversion, go on around it. Tests: three real-Postgres races in `apps/api/test/enrollments.test.ts` (each order staged, and 30 rounds sent at once), each red on the old code and again with the lock alone removed, and the re-check on PGlite, in the engine and through the route. Ride-along (T1a's review WARNs): `npm run school -- list`, each school's id, agreement day, open invites and name, never a code or its hash (`listSchools`); `add` says when a school of that name, in any case, is on record already (`schoolsNamed`). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (T1c)
- **S4a** (from Phase 6's S4) Sign out on the portal: the token cleared and the Cognito session ended, so a shared classroom computer never opens the last account — ✅ `PortalBar` (`apps/web/src/components/portal-bar.tsx`), in the root layout on every page but `/login` and `/auth/*`, the invite-code screen's included: Sign out forgets the token, then sends the browser to the hosted UI's `/logout` (`client_id`, `logout_uri` = the callback's origin + `/login`, `endSession` in `apps/web/src/lib/auth.ts`), which comes back to `/login`, "You're signed out." With no hosted UI configured, the token is still gone and `/login` says the sign-in page may still remember you. 🔧 Owner: dev's web client must list `http://localhost:3000/login` as an allowed sign-out URL (`docs/WEB.md`; this session's AWS user can't read the client to confirm). Ride-alongs (T2's review WARNs): a 429 says "a moment" or "a minute" by its `Retry-After`; `event_id_conflict` in words; the reports fallback's `restarted: false`. 👀 screenshots: the bar on the classes page and the invite screen, and the signed-out page. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (S4a)
- **E1** 💻 Exit check on dev: a class run with the simulator, its recap card and its reports page, with the rate limits on — ✅ passed 2026-10-05: the remote exit demo ran green on dev with the rate limits on, and the conductor checked the recap card and the reports page in a signed-in portal (screenshots in `~/bali-notes/e1/`)

### Phase 6 steps (security and compliance; one PR each; 👀 = the owner looks at screenshots, 📱 = needs the owner's iPhone, 🔧 = the owner in a console, ⚖️ = needs the lawyer or the school)

Added 2026-10-04 from two investigations, one of the code and one of the law and Apple's
terms (`docs/DECISIONS.md`, 2026-10-04). It runs after Phase 4 and gates Phase 5's
student-facing steps: no external TestFlight tester, no student on prod and no App Store
review until every item marked **gate** is done. S1 runs early, inside Phase 4. Each step is
one PR, under ~400 changed lines not counting tests; a step that grows past that is split.

Security:
- **S1** The authorization matrix — runs in Phase 4 (see there) — **gate** — ✅
- **S2** Log redaction: the auth header, the internal key and a database error's parameters (names, Cognito ids, unlock reasons — Drizzle's errors carry them) never reach the logs — **gate** — ✅ `apps/api/src/redact.ts`: pino's `redact` censors the three secret headers (and a cookie) in any logged headers object; the `err` serializer drops `params`/`parameters` and scrubs every string (message, stack, cause, Postgres `detail`) with P1's rule, now shared by `monitoring.ts`; tests through the injected `logStream`, one a real unique violation
- **S3** API tightening: access tokens only (`token_use`), HSTS, `nosniff` and `Cache-Control: no-store`, a length cap on `tagId`, a longer minimum `INTERNAL_API_KEY`, and a cap on the rate limiter's in-memory key map — ✅ `createVerifier` refuses any token but `token_use: 'access'` (`401`) and reads only its `client_id`; `apps/api/src/headers.ts` sets HSTS and `nosniff` on every answer and `no-store` on `/v1`'s, spread into the hijacked stream's own headers too; `TAG_ID_MAX_LENGTH` (200) shared by taps and blocks; `INTERNAL_API_KEY` `min(32)`; each budget's map capped at `MAX_KEYS` (100,000), the key idle longest evicted. Dev's key is 64 characters (owner-confirmed)
- **S4** 👀 Portal: security headers and a CSP (its Sign out landed in Phase 4, S4a) — **gate** — ✅ `contentSecurityPolicy` (`apps/web/src/lib/csp.ts`), set per request with a fresh nonce by `apps/web/src/middleware.ts` (pages render per request so Next stamps it on its scripts): `script-src 'self' 'nonce-…' 'strict-dynamic'`, `style-src 'self' 'nonce-…'` plus `style-src-attr 'unsafe-inline'` for Next's built-in 404 and error pages, `connect-src` the API, Cognito and Sentry ingest origins from the build's `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`, `form-action 'self'` and Cognito, `frame-ancestors 'none'`, `base-uri 'self'`, `object-src 'none'`; `next dev` adds eval and inline styles. HSTS, `nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy` and `Permissions-Policy` from `next.config.ts`'s `headers()`. Checked in a browser against the L2a harness: Cognito's token exchange, the classes page, the live grid's stream, a Sentry report and the reports page, with no violation. Every route must render per request (a static one has no nonce, so a blank page): `apps/web/src/app/rendering.test.ts` and CI's build step fail on one. 🔧 Owner, before the Vercel flip (and after a Next upgrade): sign in on the deployed portal, open a class's live grid and its reports with the browser console open, and check no "Content Security Policy" error shows; the policy has no report endpoint, and Sentry never sees a violation. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (S4)
- **S5** CI hardening: actions pinned by SHA, job timeouts, `npm audit --omit=dev` and `npm audit signatures` as a gate; the load gate's flood check gets a ceiling on the flooder's `200`s and a `dropped_iterations` bound (L2b's review) — ✅ actions pinned by commit with the version in a comment (the Claude workflows stay S6's), a `timeout-minutes` on every job; `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high` and `npm audit signatures` in CI's check job; `gate.js`: the flooder's `200`s at most its budget's 245, `maxVUs` 50 and `dropped_iterations` `count==0`. Rode along: #208's review (the privacy-manifest check strips `//` only outside strings, its test pins no line number, an extension declaring collected data fails, TestFlight's Release guard lints each bundle's manifest) and #210's (CI serves the built portal and checks every `<script>` on `/login` carries the response CSP's nonce, `web-csp-nonce.sh` with its self-test; the route-table check fails on a table with no `+ First Load JS` end)
- **S6** The Claude workflows: the action pinned by SHA, and the review file scanned for secrets before it posts (merging it takes the owner's ruleset toggle, `docs/GOTCHAS.md`) — ✅ merged as #270: `actions/checkout` and `anthropics/claude-code-action` pinned by commit with the version in a comment (v4.4.0, the SHA the other workflows pin; v1.0.243, the release `v1` pointed at on 2026-10-06), in `claude-review.yml` and `claude.yml`; before posting, `claude-review.yml` scans `claude-review.md` with `.github/scripts/review-secrets.py` (the PR's base commit's copy, set aside before the reviewer runs) and, on an AWS key id, a private key block with a body, a GitHub, Anthropic or Slack token, a JWT, or one of this repository's secret names with a real value, withholds it with a note on the PR and fails, the log naming the kind and line, never the text; `review-secrets.test.py` runs in CI's check job. No dependabot config to update (the repo has none for github-actions). The PR's own Claude Review is red by design (GOTCHAS). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (S6)
- **S7** Abuse tests, in process: join-code guessing, a flood of orphan unlocks, another student's `event_id`, malformed tokens (`alg: none`, HS256 signed with the public key, an unknown key id) answered `401`, never `500`, and oversized bodies; and an HTTP test that a `429` from join-code tries gives back the held miss — after L1 — ✅ `apps/api/test/abuse.test.ts`, real tokens from the test issuer: join-code guessing at production's budgets (20 tries an account, then the address's 100 misses across fresh accounts; the preview and the join), and a tries `429` giving back its held miss; 150 orphan unlocks from one account record its 120, the rest `429`, then 2 a second; another student's `event_id` on a tap, an unlock, a refocus and an invite redeem `409 event_id_conflict` with no row written; 26 tokens (`alg: none` three ways, HS256/HS512 keyed with the public key, an unknown, missing or numeric key id, another key, an embedded `jwk`, an unknown `crit`, expired, not yet valid, wrong issuer, client or audience, no subject, a swapped payload, malformed shapes) each `401`; a body over 1 MiB `413`, a capped field `400`, deep or broken JSON a `4xx`, all in the one shape; every UUID path parameter `400` (and the router's own refusals, below). Found: a parameter past 100 characters (`414`) or badly percent-encoded (`400`) left in Fastify's shape, echoing the path; fixed by `frameworkErrors` (`routerRefusal`, `apps/api/src/errors.ts`). The ID-token check is `it.todo` until S3 (#209). Why and what's left: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (S7)
- **S8** 🔧 Least-privilege database roles: the API's runtime role cannot change the schema or update, delete or truncate `events`; migrations run as their own role
- **S9** Clock skew surfaced, as ARCHITECTURE's iOS rules already promise: a note on the record when a device's time is far from the server's, and a badge on the grid — ✅ `insertEvent` adds `clock_ahead_s` (whole seconds) to the payload of a tap, refocus, Screen Time on or off, or unlock (orphans included) whose claimed time runs more than `CLOCK_AHEAD_THRESHOLD_MS` (2 min, `@bali/shared`) ahead of when the server heard it; never for a claim behind (an offline catch-up looks the same); advisory, the clamp and A10–A14 untouched. `getSessionRoster` and the snapshot carry `clockOff` (additive); the grid's chip shows "Clock off" beside the name in its own ink (`live-grid.tsx`, `grid-state.ts`). PGlite tests `packages/db/test/clock-skew.test.ts`. Not covered: pre-bell taps converted at Start, and check-ins (no history). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (S9)

- **S11** 🔧 Self sign-up gated to the school's email domains (owner ruling, 2026-10-04) — ✅ `infra/cognito/pre-signup.mjs`, a dependency-free Cognito Pre sign-up Lambda (Node 22, pasted in by the owner, `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` runbook 2 step 4): refuses an email whose domain isn't exactly one of `ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS` (comma-separated, any case; subdomains listed one by one; unset or empty refuses all), for sign-ups and first external-provider sign-ins; console-made accounts pass. `node --test` tests, run by the root `npm test`

- **C3** ⚖️ Account deletion (App Store 5.1.1(v)): `DELETE /v1/me` through the transition engine, idempotent on its `eventId` — it ends the enrollments, de-identifies the student's events, and deletes the account, its name and the Cognito sign-in (owner ruling, 2026-10-04); ARCHITECTURE's data-model decision 3, "nothing is truly deleted", is amended in this PR (ISSUES #5) — **gate** — ✅ `deleteAccount` (`packages/db/src/transitions.ts`): every class left via `endLiveEnrollment` (shared with `endEnrollment`), the `users` row kept as a tombstone (no name, `cognito_id` `deleted:<id>`, `removed_at`) so reports count as before, rename payloads emptied (migration 0015), `account_deleted` recorded with no payload; teachers with a class or block refused; the student's tap lock and row taken first, so a racing tap, join or rename is `409 account_deleted`; follow-up (#217's WARNs): the arm path reads the row FOR SHARE behind a deletion, so a racing pre-bell tap is `409 account_deleted` too, never armed for no one; a class or block create holds the teacher's row the same way, so none is owned by a deleted teacher; the deletion's `eventId` replay is scoped to its caller (another account's deletion id is `event_id_conflict` unless this account was made after it); migration 0016 nests the events trigger's UPDATE test so TRUNCATE never reads OLD. PGlite tests `packages/db/test/account-deletion.test.ts`, a real-Postgres race (deletion vs tap, unlock, rename, join and the end), HTTP tests in `me.test.ts`, fixtures `contracts/fixtures/account/`, BaliCore's `deleteMe`. The Cognito sign-in is C4's (the phone's own `DeleteUser`). `ACCOUNT_DELETION_COVERAGE` + schema test (2026-10-13): every foreign key to `users` placed by what the deletion does with its rows, so a new one fails CI until it is. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (C3; 2026-10-13, its coverage guard)
- **C4** 📱 Delete account on the Me screen: the outbox is sent first, so a queued unlock lands before the account goes — required, not assumed: the outbox (unlocks first) is drained before `DELETE /v1/me` is sent, else a late unlock lands as an orphan under a fresh account and leaves that lesson's report (#217's review); a retry answered `409 event_id_conflict` is retried once under a fresh id (a boot call racing the deletion can make the reborn row look older than it); then `DELETE /v1/me`, then Cognito's own `DeleteUser` with the phone's access token (🔧 owner first: the phone's app client allows, and the phone requests, the `aws.cognito.signin.user.admin` scope) — **gate** — **C4a** ✅ the engine, one call: `SyncEngine.deleteAccount(signIn)`, answered in `AccountDeletion` states (C4b words them). The sign-in asks for the admin scope; a token without it (a sign-in from before) is `signInFirst`, nothing sent. The loops hold and what they have on its way lands, a second press sharing the first's answer; the outbox goes, every unlock first (one under a queued tap after it), an unrecorded unlock holding the deletion back (`unlockUnsent`), a refused tap not; `DELETE /v1/me` under one id while unanswered, once more fresh on `409 event_id_conflict`, `teacher_has_classes` its own state. Deleted: the sign-in gives the API no token any more (`Tokens.deleted`, kept in the Keychain through renewals and relaunches), the account's queued records but unlocks are let go, and the phone leaves its session with no unlock (the owner's ruling); then `DeleteUser` at the pool's endpoint (the token's issuer, checked to be Cognito's), kept and retried alone until done, then the sign-in forgotten. Tests: `DeleteUserTests` (BaliCore), `DeleteAccountTests`, `DeletionOrderTests` (BaliOutbox). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (C4a). **C4b** ✅ the Me screen (the owner's picks, 2026-10-05; today's D1 look, D2 on hold): a red "Delete account" text button at the very bottom of Me, under Sign out and whose sign-in it is; pressed, it gives way to its question under it, as Leave asks ("Delete your account?", the owner's consequence line, a red Delete account and Cancel); confirmed, the deletion's own screen (`Screen.deleting`, `DeletingView`) shows over every other, Focus and its Emergency Unlock included, from the press to the end: deleting, with nothing to press; a stop that deleted nothing, said with Try again where another try can help and Back (`signInFirst` and `teacherHasClasses` get Back alone; `notDeleted` in the Join screen's words for its answer); the account deleted and its sign-in not yet, with Try again alone, at a relaunch too (`SignIn.deletionPending`, read with who is signed in); and done, OK leaving to Sign in. The rules and words are `Deleting` (`ios/BaliOutbox/Sources/BaliOutbox/Deletion.swift`, Linux-tested); a change of who is signed in drops the question or a stop and holds the rest. Tests: `DeletingTests`, `ScreenTests.deleting` (BaliOutbox); `AppTests.fixtures`, `deleteAccountWiring`, `tokens` (the destructive red pinned). Fixtures: `meDeleteAsk`, `deleting`, `deletingShielded`, `deletingSignInFirst`, `deletingUnlockUnsent`, `deletingUnread`, `deletingTeacher`, `deletingNotDeleted`, `deletingPending`, `deletingDone`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (C4b). - **C3-cognito** ✅ (2026-10-09, the owner's decision) The server deletes a deleted account's Cognito sign-in, durably, so it goes even when the phone's own DeleteUser (C4) never lands — offline at that second, then a reinstall (#299) or a new phone; the under-13 fallback's (#287) too. `cognito_deletions` (migration 0020: the access token's `iss`, `username` and `sub`, the tries and when the next is due), written beside the engine (`packages/db/src/cognito-deletions.ts`): `deleteAccount` queues it in its own transaction (its `signIn`), and `DELETE /v1/me` from a sign-in with no account here queues it from the route. The API (`apps/api/src/cognito/`) runs a pass right after the commit, in the background as the Start's push, and one in every sweep (`app.sweep`: the API's minute tick and the cron's `/internal/sweep`): up to 10 due rows of this deploy's `AUTH_ISSUER`, claimed with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, each claim counting the try and putting the next off (1, 2, 4… minutes, at most an hour). A try reads the user (AdminGetUser) and deletes it (AdminDeleteUser) only while its `sub` is the queued one; not found, or another `sub`, is done with nothing deleted; first, at every try, a live account holding the queued `sub` (its person came back and signed in again; santa's round 1, the owner's ruling) finishes the row with nothing deleted, Cognito not called (`came_back`); anything else is retried, logged at warn, from the third try at error and to Sentry, never naming the person. Signed with Signature Version 4 by Node's own crypto, no dependency. The key: `COGNITO_DELETER_ACCESS_KEY_ID` and `COGNITO_DELETER_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, both or none beside a Cognito `AUTH_ISSUER`, the pool and region read from it (`.railway/railway.ts` preserves both); unset, the queue waits and the boot warns. No change to the `/v1` surface, the OpenAPI snapshot or the fixtures. Tests: `packages/db/test/cognito-deletions.test.ts`, a real-Postgres race (two claims at once), `apps/api/test/cognito-admin.test.ts` (AWS's published signing vector, and the SDK signer's), `sign-in-deletion.test.ts`, `env.test.ts`. 🔧 The owner: runbook 9 (an IAM policy, user and key per environment, the two Railway variables, a check on dev). C6a and C6b are unchanged; the disposal queuing each person's sign-in by `sub` is proposed as its own step. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-09, C3-cognito)
- **C5** One student's whole record, exported for a parent's inspection request (FERPA; a data agreement gives the vendor days, not weeks) — ✅ `npm run school -- export-student <id or Cognito sub>` (`exportStudentRecord`, `packages/db/src/student-record.ts`): one JSON document (`bali.student-record/1`) on standard output — the account, every enrollment, participation, event, armed tap and redeemed invite keyed to them, and the names of the classes, sessions and school they point at; nothing of another student's; one read-only transaction. `STUDENT_RECORD_COVERAGE` places every foreign key to `users`, and a test fails on one neither exported nor argued out. No endpoint: the owner answers through the school. Runbook: `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` 1, step 10 (prod via `railway ssh`, the hold on disposals, delivery). Rode along: `deleteAccount` consumes the student's waiting armed taps. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (C5)
- **C6a** A command that disposes of one school's data on its written request, logged without personal data — ✅ `npm run school -- dispose <school-id> [--confirm "<name>"]` (`disposeSchool`, `packages/db/src/transitions.ts`): a preview without the name (the disposal run and rolled back); with it, one transaction: every person of the school (its teachers; anyone enrolled in, present in or recorded in its classes) de-identified as C3 leaves an account, enrollments ended, classes removed and nameless, blocks removed, open invites and pre-bell taps deleted, the school marked removed; lessons, participations and events stay under rows naming no one; a `school_disposed` event of the school's id and counts. Refused while a lesson runs (one past its bell is ended as the sweep would) or a person has records at another school; idempotent; `startSession` refuses a removed class. `SCHOOL_DISPOSAL_COVERAGE` + schema test; PGlite tests `packages/db/test/school-disposal.test.ts`, a real-Postgres race. Runbook: `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` 1, step 11 (prod, Cognito sign-ins, backups, writing back). Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (C6a)

Runbook 1, step 12 (the calendar entry). - **C7** 📱 13+ is the school's agreement plus a neutral in-app age screen that stores nothing when the answer is no (owner ruling, 2026-10-04); it matters for the later K-12 pilot, since the Vanderbilt pilot's users are adults — **gate** — ✅ the screen (2026-10-05, the owner's picks), asked at every Sign up (since 2026-10-07, the owner's ruling: no account exists yet to have passed it, and a second person never signs up unasked) — before the intro and the sign-up page (since 2026-10-07, the approved Sign in & sign up design; at Sign in, after the intro, from 2026-10-06; the first screen on a first launch before that) — "When were you born?", the birth month and year from two menus that start on "Choose" and offer the twelve months and this year with a hundred before it, leaving out the future, so nothing says 13, hints at the cutoff or preselects an answer, and no pick can be invalid; Continue once both are picked, which at 13 or older goes on at once to the intro, or the sign-up page, Continue saying "Signing up…" while it opens (`ios/Bali/UI/AgeView.swift`). The rule, `AgeCheck` in BaliOutbox: 13 or older only once the month after the birth month has begun, 13 years on, so a 12-year-old never passes; passed is kept per account, never the date (since 2026-10-07; before, one phone-wide flag, `ageChecked`, now no longer read, so it vouches for no account): the per-account part below; under 13 keeps nothing anywhere — in memory for the run — and lands on the stop screen ("Bali isn't available for you yet. Ask your teacher how to take part in class without the app.") until the app is reopened. `Phone.signIn(_:through:)` is where every way to the hosted UI starts — Sign in's two buttons and the Debug readout; the age screen's Continue and the intro's Sign up carry a Sign up on, through the private `Phone.open`, only after a 13 or older answer that same time (since 2026-10-07) — though Cognito's own sign-in page links to its sign-up, which nothing in the classic hosted UI can hide for the app alone (the gap; its fallback, below). The router (`Screen.choose`) shows the question and the stop screen in Sign in's place, and the shields' Focus and the home a standing not read keeps, which hold Emergency Unlock, come before them. Counted in the Gregorian calendar whatever calendar the phone shows (santa's round 1: thirteen Islamic years would pass a 12-year-old). Tests: `AgeTests` and `ScreenTests.ageAtSignIn` (BaliOutbox), `AppTests.ageCheck` and `AppTests.signUpRouted`; fixtures `age`, `agePicked`, `tooYoung`; screenshots `~/bali-notes/c7/`, the Sign in flow's in the PR moving it. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-05, C7; 2026-10-06, at Sign in). 📱 still the owner's, on the next TestFlight build: a first launch on the iPhone (the checks are on the second half's PR). **The gap's fallback** ✅ (2026-10-07; the owner's decision of 2026-10-06, the first half of the approved [Sign in & sign up design](https://claude.ai/artifact/McyYhpX1UTq1nU6oCCwPwG)): a sign-in into an account that has not passed the check on this phone — made around the question on Cognito's pages, or an existing account new to the phone (since 2026-10-07) — gets the question first, in the router before Screen Time and Home, never over a session's screens (Focus, Unlocked, Protection off, Session over not closed) nor the home a standing not read keeps; and Bali's API gets no token from it until it is answered: `SignIn`'s `cleared`, asked with the account's Cognito id and read from the phone's own defaults at each ask (`ios/Bali/BaliApp.swift`; since 2026-10-08 the sign-in's own `Tokens.checked`, C7-server's app half), so the engine's sends, reads and check-ins and the screens' own calls find none, kept, never dropped. 13 or older sends everything at once (`Phone.answerAge`, `retryNow`). Under 13 deletes the account by Delete account's own steps (`SyncEngine.deleteAccount`, C4; Claude Review's blocker on the first version, which sent nothing queued): what the phone queued goes first, every Emergency Unlock first and one the server has not recorded holding the deletion back, then `DELETE /v1/me`, then Cognito's DeleteUser, then the rest of the outbox let go, so nothing is ever filed under whoever signs in next; all under the sign-in's deletion token (`TokenProvider.deletionToken`, `APIClient.forDeletion`), given whatever the check, so Me's Delete account works under the gate too. With nothing queued the server never sees the account (`DELETE /v1/me` makes none, `already_deleted`); a record queued under a sign-in it never saw makes the account as it lands, de-identified a moment later by the deletion. Its screen (`Deleting.saidUnderThirteen`): the canvas's words under the stop screen's title, on its gutter, where the canvas drew them (deleting; a deletion that could not reach Bali, with Try again; deleted, final for the run), and Back to Focus on a stop while the shields are on, as C4b's stops have it (santa's round 1); every other stop, a server's own answer among them, in C4b's words and ways on (`answered(_:underThirteen:)`), Back where no try can help; C4b's own screens as approved; the fallback's words only for the deletion an answer under 13 starts, never Me's. Known limits: until C7-server's app half (2026-10-08, below), an existing student on a new phone answered it once, and so did each account on a phone the first time it signed in there, a reinstall's included; now only an account Bali's server holds no yes for is asked — one whose yes never reached it, its sign-in signed out first, once more; a phone signed in on a build from before the question per account (2026-10-07), its account not in build 8's note, is asked at its next launch out of a session, its API held till then, so an Emergency Unlock it makes in class reaches the teacher only after (the owner's ruling to change); C4b's line for a sign-in from before C4's scope names Sign out, which the stop screen doesn't offer. Tests: `AgeFallbackTests`, `ScreenTests.ageAfterSignIn`, `DeletingTests.underThirteen` (BaliOutbox), `SignInTests.cleared` and `APIClientTests.deletionToken` (BaliCore), `AppTests.ageAfterSignIn`, `ageAfterSignInWiring`, `deleteFromMeUnchecked`, `deletingLayout` and `deletingUnderThirteenShielded`, and `me.test.ts`'s never-called `DELETE /v1/me`; fixtures `ageAfterSignIn`, `deletingUnderThirteen`, `deletingUnderThirteenNotDeleted`, `deletingUnderThirteenDone`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-07, Sign in & sign up). **The Sign in screen and the sign-up path** ✅ (2026-10-07, the design's second half): a first launch opens on Sign in, "Sign up or sign in" (each way's two words on one line, as the canvas keeps them), "You only do this once. After that, Bali remembers you.", Sign up filled and Sign in outlined (`ios/Bali/UI/SignInView.swift`); while a page opens its button says "Signing up…" or "Signing in…" and neither takes a press; a page that could not open is said under the buttons, naming its page ("The sign-up page couldn't open. Try again, or ask your teacher."; `SignInError.words(on:)`); the largest text size scrolls. Sign up (`Phone.signIn(.signUp, through:)`) asks the 13+ question, every time since 2026-10-07, then shows the intro where this run has not shown it (`introShows`, in Sign in's place; the intro's last button now Sign up, "Signing up…" while its page opens, `Phone.sawIntro(through:)`), then opens Cognito's sign-up page, the classic hosted UI's `/signup` with the sign-in's client, scope, redirect and PKCE (`HostedPage`, `SignIn.signIn(_:through:)`), and the account it makes, nameless in production, gets Your name next, then Screen Time and Home (Go-live features, the onboarding row); Sign in opens Cognito's sign-in page at once, no question, no intro. The intro seen is this run's alone (`introSeen`, in memory since 2026-10-07: once per account, not per phone, the owner's ruling), so Sign up again this run, the question asked again, opens its page from the question's Continue, and a later run's shows the intro first; a page that closes or could not open lands on Sign in, and one that signs the student in keeps the intro until the sign-in lands, so no Sign in shows between; after an answer under 13 no page opens that run. Dev's `/signup` was opened from the app in the simulator; no account was made there. Tests: `SignInTests.authorize`, `SignInTests.signUp` (BaliCore), `ScreenTests` (the router: Sign in first, Sign up's question and intro; `words`), `AppTests.signUpRouted`, `signUpAndSignIn`, `signUpLands`, `signInScreen`, `introSeen`, `policyLinks`; fixtures `signInSigningIn`, `signInSigningUp`, `signInNotOpened`, `signInSignUpNotOpened`, `intro`, `introSigningUp`. Screenshots beside the canvas: `~/bali-notes/app-signin/part2/side-by-side/`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-07, the Sign in screen and the sign-up path). **The question per account** ✅ (2026-10-07; the owner's rulings, the approved design's version 8; where the phone kept "passed", superseded 2026-10-08 by C7-server's app half, below: the yes is the server's, and the phone keeps no age note): passed is kept per account, the Cognito ids of the accounts that passed on this phone in its own defaults (`AgeCheck.key`, `ageCheckedAccounts`; `AgeCheck.passed`, `AgeCheck.pass`), the id read from the sign-in's own token (its `sub`), no call to Bali's API; never the date, and nothing for an answer under 13. Every Sign up asks it, a second this run too (`Phone.signIn(.signUp, through:)`); a 13 or older answer vouches for the account its page signs in — made there, or one signed into from it — filed as the sign-in lands, before a token is given, a watcher hears or the engine is told (`SignIn.signIn`'s `landing`), so it is never asked again on this phone; the intro seen this run, Continue opens the page at once, the question kept with "Signing up…" on Continue and its picks until the sign-in lands (`Phone.signed`), as the design's Age board draws it. A page that ends with no sign-in lets the answer and the picks go, so the next Sign up asks again. Sign in skips it only for an account that passed on this phone (`Phone.ageNow`, the router's input, read from the defaults, a fixture's its own); any other account gets #287's fallback, the account filed once answered 13 or older there. The phone-wide flag builds before kept vouches for no account: an existing account signed in across the update is asked once, at its next launch out of a session, its API held till then. Tests: `SignInTests.cleared`, `landing` (BaliCore), `AgeTests.keeps` (BaliOutbox), `AppTests.agePerAccount`, `signUpLands`, `signUpRouted`, `signUpAndSignIn`, `ageCheck`, `ageContinue`, `introSeen`, `introAfterSignOut`, `ageAfterSignInWiring`; fixture `ageSigningUp`. Screenshots beside the canvas: `~/bali-notes/app-home-age/part2/`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-07, the question per account)
- **C7-server** ✅ the backend and the app's half (2026-10-08, the owner's decision): the 13+ yes kept per account on the server, so Sign up or sign in can always be the app's first screen and a student is never asked again on a new phone or after a reinstall. `age_checks` (migration 0019; `user_id` its key, `event_id` unique, `created_at` by the server's clock): only that the account confirmed 13 or older, never a birth date or an age; nothing under 13. Written beside the engine (`packages/db/src/age-checks.ts`). **The interface, for the Mac session:** `GET /v1/me/age-check` → `200 { passed }`, a read that makes no account (none here, a deleted one's too: `false`; a teacher's: `true`, never asked); `PUT /v1/me/age-check` `{ eventId }` (a fresh UUIDv7) → `200 { passed: true }`, idempotent on it, the account's row made as the boot call makes it, a teacher's recording nothing. Refusals: `400` (bad body), `401`, `409 event_id_conflict` (the `eventId` another account's yes holds), and a race-only `409 account_deleted`. BaliCore: `APIClient.ageCheck()` and `APIClient.recordAgeCheck(_: RecordAgeCheckRequest)`, both answering `AgeCheckResponse` (`passed`). Fixtures: `contracts/fixtures/age-check/`. Personal data: C3 deletes it, and so do C6a and C6b for each account they de-identify (a continuing one keeps it); C5 exports it as `ageCheck`; the three coverage maps place it. Privacy: no new App Store data type, it rides Other Data Types (⚖️ confirm; `docs/APP-STORE.md`). Tests: `apps/api/test/age-check.test.ts`, the matrix's rows, `account-deletion`, `school-disposal`, `retention` and `student-record`, and a real-Postgres race (a yes beside a deletion, both orders). **The app's half** ✅ (2026-10-08; the approved design's flow map, version 10): the app reads and records the server's yes, and the phone keeps no age note. The gate is the sign-in's own (`SignIn`, `gated`): Bali's API gets no token until it is through the check (`Tokens.checked`, in the Keychain with the tokens, kept through a renewal and a relaunch, gone with a sign-out or a reinstall), but the check's own read and a deletion's (`deletionToken`, `APIClient.forDeletion`). A sign-in not through it — a Sign in made this run, or one kept from the last — asks `GET /v1/me/age-check` (`SyncEngine.ageCheck`, `Phone.askAge`), the starting mark meanwhile (`AgeCheck.Answer.checking`, the router's `.starting`); passed, a teacher's too, the sign-in is let through (`SignIn.passed`) and goes on; not passed, or Bali not reached (the owner's pick: the safe side, no new screen), the question; 13 or older keeps a yes with the sign-in (`passed(_:recording:)`), as a Sign up's sign-in lands with one (`SignIn.signIn`'s `yes`). The engine's read loop sends it, `PUT /v1/me/age-check` under its one event id, at each wake until answered, counted and never during an account deletion (a yes landing after `DELETE /v1/me` would make a fresh account); a refusal for good (a 4xx but 401, 408, 429) lets it go. Under 13 sends nothing, and deletes the account as before. Build 8's note, once: at the first launch with the Keychain readable, a yes `ageCheckedAccounts` holds for the account signed in is kept to send, then the list and the older `ageChecked` flag are deleted, whoever is signed in (`AgeCheck.forgetNotes`). Tests: `SignInTests.checked`, `signUpYes` (BaliCore); `AgeFallbackTests` (the gate, under 13, the yes recorded, kept, refused, and ahead of a deletion), `AgeTests.keeps`, `ScreenTests` (BaliOutbox); `AppTests.ageFromServer` (passed, a teacher's, no yes, Bali not reached, under 13), `ageNoteMigrated`, `ageAfterSignInWiring`, `signUpLands`, `deleteFromMeUnchecked`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-08, C7-server; and the app's half). **Asked again in class** ✅ (2026-10-09; #300's review, the owner's approval): a sign-in whose ask could not reach Bali, the question unable to show over Focus or a session's screens, is asked again at each of the engine's wakes (`SyncEngine.askAgeAtEachWake`, `Phone.askAgeAgain`; never during an account deletion), so a yes lets it through before the bell and its held sends, an Emergency Unlock's too, go at once; no yes stops the asking, nothing sent, the question once the session is over; out of a session the question shows and nothing is asked. No new screen, words or endpoint; a network coming back is found at the next wake (no network watcher). Tests: `AgeFallbackTests.askAgeAtEachWake` (BaliOutbox), `AppTests.ageAgainInClass`. Why: `docs/DECISIONS.md` (2026-10-09)

The finished steps' full lines; the open steps and this section's intro are in `docs/PLAN.md`.

And two corrections: ClassEnded's line under "Focused the whole time" ("Tapped in by 9:08 AM and focused until the bell.") against the owner's rule that a late tap-in still counts (DESIGN.md §1), and ReportOpened's recap card, still titled "Last session", whose "Open its report" links to its own page — validation: the owner's approval on the canvas, dated in `docs/DECISIONS.md`; a step building one of these waits for it — ✅ approved by the owner (2026-10-12; `docs/DECISIONS.md`): the classes home's card gone; L7c's Questions on the report page only; a teacher's page at phone width as a class live (two columns of short tiles) and Home, Present and Recent activity hidden under 640 px; Present after the bell "Class is over" alone; the rest as drawn
